> import re
> import sys
>
> file_path = '/app/varsea/disks/sub1/subA/subAlpha/ae3f4c.dat'
> # Read the file as binary
> with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
>     data = f.read()
>
> # Convert to string (ASCII, ignore errors)
> data_str = data.decode('ascii', errors='ignore')
>
> # Search for 'PASSWORD='
> pos = data_str.find('PASSWORD=')
> passwords = []
>
> if pos != -1:
>     # Extract the substring after 'PASSWORD='
>     candidate = data_str[pos+8:]
>     # Check if candidate is alphanumeric and length 23
>     if len(candidate) == 23 and candidate.isalnum() and candidate.startswith('8XD') and candidate.endswith('W54'):
>         passwords.append(candidate)
>     else:
>         # Maybe the candidate is longer, we need to find a 23-character alphanumeric substring
>         # that starts with '8XD' and ends with 'W54' and is within the candidate
>         # We'll search for patterns of length 23 within the candidate
>         for i in range(len(candidate) - 23 + 1):
>             sub = candidate[i:i+23]
>             if sub.isalnum() and sub.startswith('8XD') and sub.endswith('W54'):
>                 passwords.append(sub)
>                 break
>
> # Also scan the entire string for 23-character alphanumeric strings

import re
import struct

file_path = '/app/varsea/disks/sub1/subA/subAlpha/ae3f4c.dat'
with open(file_path, 'rb') as f:
    data = f.read()

# Convert to string for regex
try:
    data_str = data.decode('ascii', errors='ignore')
except:
    data_str = ''

# Find all 23-character alphanumeric strings
pattern = re.compile(r'[A-Z0-9]{23}')
matches = list(pattern.finditer(data_str))
print(f'Found {len(matches)} 23-char alphanumeric strings')
for match in matches:
    cand = match.group(0)
    if cand.startswith('8XD'):
        print(f'  Startswith 8XD: {cand}')
        if cand.endswith('W54'):
            print('  Endswith W54!')
        # print the surrounding 50 characters
        start = match.start() - 50
        end = match.end() + 50
        context = data_str[start:end]
        print(f'    Context: {context}')

# Also search for 'PASSWORD=' in the raw bytes
pattern = b'PASSWORD='
pos = data.find(pattern)
if pos != -1:
    print(f'Found PASSWORD= at position {pos}')
    print(f'  Raw bytes after: {data[pos:pos+200]}')
    # Print the string after as ASCII
    s = data[pos+8:pos+8+200]
    print(f'  ASCII: {s.decode("ascii", errors="ignore")}')
EOF
